- The release stays per payment through today's Run payables. The weekly ISO-week payment cycle (US-10.2.7, OQ-47 Proposed) is Phase 39a's: no copy in this phase names a payment day, a week number or a weekly cycle.
- BCTIs are counted in exactly one place: bctisFor (pure, in src/domain/billing/bcti.ts, with tests) over bctiRecords (one selector, one record per procedure ACCPAY, the pre-payment invoice's included, with its stored anaesthetist, issue date and its receivable's paid date). One BCTI per receivable invoice, each counted once against the anaesthetist who did the procedure, and only once its receivable is paid in full, in the month it is paid (so no paid BCTI is missed or charged twice). The paid-only rule is one switch (BCTI_COUNT_RULE.paidOnly); one comment beside the function and the Admin caption are the only places that say the count is being confirmed with AA's accountant (OQ-60, OQ-29). Later feeders are 22, 36, 38b, 39 and 39b. No other code counts BCTIs, including the demo trigger's disabled check.
- The monthly run raises one AA-FEE invoice per active anaesthetist per month (fixed items plus per-BCTI charge times the count), refuses a future month, and is a no-op on a rerun. The acceptance example must reproduce: $500 + $5 x 40 = $700 before GST.
- The AA fee invoice is its own case in billing.aaFeeInvoices, outside billing.cases, with a kind:'aaFee' ACCREC against the anaesthetist's existing Xero contact and no ACCPAY, raised through one exported raiseAnaesthetistInvoiceInto path, the one path for an invoice from AA to an anaesthetist (Phase 36 gives it its ledger pair; nothing is built for OQ-71's negative with no later payment, which is handled outside the system). It is always a separate invoice paid into AA's own account and never netted: no code path deducts it from a payable, a payables run or a disbursement (test it). It must never reach payables, GST activity, Overdue, the monitor pipeline, the Payment received webhook triggers (hide them on a fee pair with a `when`), the reconciliation poll or the archive job, and a fee payment creates no BillingReceipt.
- InvoiceNumber and Reference are stored on both Xero records, not derived at view time; the ACCPAY number keeps the -P suffix; the ACCREC's paidAtISO is stamped once by the completing payment; no NHI crosses to Xero.
- Every write goes through mutate() with an audit entry naming the right actor (office button, scheduled system run, Xero payment, settings save). Time comes from the demo clock only.
- Demo triggers go through the Phase 14 registry, shown only on their screen, surfaces bar, with disabled states, and bodies in src/store or src/shared: "Run scheduled month-end fee run" and "Seed a month of BCTIs" (tops Dr Rutherford up to exactly 40 receivable invoices paid in full in July 2026 and disbursed, with service dates 1, 2, 3 and 6 July, before the canvas horizon start, so no added List collides with a generated canvas List; the $700 example then reproduces under the paid-only count) on Admin AA fee invoices, and "Record fee payment" on a fee pair in the Xero simulation. "Run monthly fee invoices" is a product office button on the AA fee invoices screen, not a demo trigger. The re-homed Payment received Full and Half triggers now release exactly the amount received and are hidden on a fee pair; Phase 14's PWA pwa-payment-full / pwa-payment-half on Mobile · Balances share that body, so they release exactly too and lose any fee wording. The registry (src/shared/demoTriggers/) may import only from src/store and src/domain, so seed-scoped entries name ANAE constants and the screen publishes its month through useDemoTriggerContext. Nothing is added to the Control Panel page. No new PWA entry is needed; confirm none of this phase's new entries show in the PWA sheet.
- Do not re-litigate the PROGRESS.md Decisions log, except the 2026-07-29 fee ruling this phase supersedes.
- Follow the design files: teal is the only action colour, crimson stays identity only, pills use semantic tokens, amounts are mono with tabular-nums. No en or em dashes in any app copy.

- Funding tags: SXAP, HNZ, ACC in step-rail order, each optionally naming its funder Party (our reading); add, edit, move and remove, audited; a tag a Contract carries cannot be removed. Nothing reads the funder yet (21's "Suggested").
- Contracts: specialtyIds (none means every specialty) and at most one fundingTagId, validated in 18's one save-guard function. Seed tags and specialties only where they leave every seeded Procedure's Contract fitting its List's surgeon (a test asserts it), per the doc's table. The filter itself, the Other price toggle and the destination step are 20's: do not build them, and add no "show all".
- Fallbacks: ensureFallbacks is one pure, idempotent function (the seed gives it deterministic ids from the destination key, the store allocateId), run after seeding and inside the same mutate() as every Contract create, edit and re-enable, one audit meta per fallback made. Kind open exists only on fallbacks: the office cannot choose it; system Contracts (No contract · RVG and every fallback) accept only the allowed-fields constant (name and AA code) and are never disabled. In code openPriceFallback, never fixedFallback. Every reader that scans Contracts (the interim resolver, the route's holder scan, search, counts) must skip or label a kind open Contract: a fallback must never be chosen or silently counted.
- ACC is a funding tag: the ACC Contracts carry it and keep their rate; Procedure.accRelated is deleted and the ACC chip and column read procedureAccFunded from the Contract's tag; no ACC branch in pricing.
- Determinism: never Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random(); seed timestamps are fixed strings. Every write goes through mutate() with an audit entry (names, never raw ids or undefined); guards live in the store, not the UI; components never own domain state. Bump PERSIST_VERSION once from the value you find, with a comment naming this phase.

The billing run, 20a's whoIsInvoicedFor, the invoice and Xero all read it; 20's interim and any routeCounterpartyForHolder go. It never reads a price or the funder. Delivery (invoiceDeliveryFor: the Party's setting, else the payer's email, else post) lives beside it and 22 sends from it. Age for the child warning reuses 19b's ageInCompletedYears.
- Every Booking names a payer: Booking.payer is the patient (the "different payer" tick clear, details read from the patient record) or another party who pays (ticked: name, relationship, email, NZ Post postal address, a BP#### id), such as a parent, guardian, benefactor or family trust. Set to the patient on every creation path; changed only through setBookingPayer (the anaesthetist on their own List and the office until AUTHORISED). The guardian master goes (createBillableParty, Procedure.billablePartyId, the BP0001 guardian row, "New guardian"); Hana Park becomes the payer on Grace Park's Booking, keeping BP0001 and its Xero contact name. A billed payer needs an email or a postal address: the payer sheet asks and a Review flag shows it, never a completion block (Review's full layout is 21a's).
- The funder is two optional Booking fields (funderPartyId, a Party of kind insurer or funder, and insuranceApprovalNumber), written only by setBookingInsurance (the office and the anaesthetist under editRefusal), set from intake where a path carries them. One mild warning rule: approvalNumberMissing (the funder is an insurer, NIB or Southern Cross Health Society, and there is no number), never for ACC or Health NZ, the only insurance warning, shown to the office and the anaesthetist. There is no insurer-with-no-insurer-Contract rule, and 15a's stale sample text naming it in registry.ts and warningSamples.ts goes. The AIA Booking on No contract · RVG stays, billed to the patient, with no warning. Nothing blocks, and the picker is not filtered or tagged.
- The suggested path (US-04.3.10) sits inside 20's one path function: no funder, Patient › Standard; a funder that holds a Contract offered on the List, Special › that holder; otherwise the List's hospital under the funding tag whose funderPartyId is the funder (add that field to the tag if 18a left none); else nothing. - The invoice keeps its recipe (US-08.4.4): a locked invoice's document prints its addressee, patient and supplier from the lock, and Regenerate rebuilds it from the stored inputs. Nothing rebuilds an invoice from data as it stood at another point in time.

- Contract history (US-04.1.3): a History tab on Phase 18's Contract panel shows the audited edits (create, edit, disable, price ranges, specialties, tag) with who, when and values before and after, and the Procedures locked against the Contract with their invoices; Parties and RVG groups get the same view through one shared component only where 18 and 19 did not already show it. Nothing is deleted.
- Seeded AUTHORISED Lists get locks at seed time (migrated locks for the history backdrop, labelled, payee stamped). No new RNG draws. Bump PERSIST_VERSION once.
- Locked Bookings display from the lock in all three apps (fees, the stack's Contract part, Review) with the neutral "Contract locked at authorise" badge; ACTIVE Bookings still re-price live.
- Triggers: register "Regenerate from locked data" (harness bar, Admin invoice document) and re-point Phase 14's "Trigger billing failure" (harness bar, Billing monitor) to a Xero hand-off fault scoped to Losa Tuilagi's one-invoice Booking (no holder reference exists to go missing, D82), with its body stageBillingFailure in src/store/demoStaging.ts so pwaPurity holds. They must be deterministic, with a disabled state; regenerate is read-only and keeps its result in Phase 14's non-persisted trigger memory. No new PWA entry, but test that the PWA office stand-in locks the List with the handset's anaesthetist as payee. Nothing goes on the Control Panel page beyond the S4 and S5 scenario text. The History tab and the backdated price range are product UI, no button.
- House rules: every write goes through mutate(), and every new audit code gets a label in src/shared/audit/actionLabels.ts; keep billing rules pure in src/domain/billing with Vitest tests; teal is the only action colour, crimson is identity only, the lock badge is a neutral pill; admin uses panels and overlays, and any price an anaesthetist sees on a locked Booking is read-only; no en or em dashes in app copy; never "slot" (say session, AM or PM); "Booking", never "card". Never commit.

- Generation follows US-01.1.1 (Confirmed) and OQ-81 part 1: only active anaesthetists, from their start date; every new Slot written free; then the anaesthetist's calendar; then recurring bookings. The RNG fill goes on every generated date (rolled days, new anaesthetists, horizon extensions), weekends included. Recurring bookings keep today's weekday-only guard (Phase 30 adds weekend patterns). A recurring booking that meets an unavailable Slot is traced as a clash, not projected (Phase 31 makes it a Draft List).
- The horizon is a setting (FT-01.1): appSettings.schedule.horizonMonths, 4 by default, beside the default AM and PM times, in one schedule-settings record. Every horizon reader goes through horizonFor(today, months). Extending generates the new days; shortening drops far-edge Slots and empty Lists and refuses while a List there holds Bookings.
- Determinism first. Before touching the generator, commit a golden fixture of today's canvas (the demo window 2026-07-07 to 2026-08-16 and the far edge) and seeded Bookings. The seed keeps today's slot-RNG content inside that fixed window as recorded history (seedRecordedWindow, same per-slot draws), so the window and every Booking reproduce exactly; the far-edge section is regenerated once, deliberately, when the RNG fill goes. Projected Lists keep their L-<reg>-<date>-<session> ids (never parsed to find a Slot); runtime Lists take LG####; Slots are S-<reg>-<date>-<session>. Bump PERSIST_VERSION by one and extend the migrate test.
- Start date and active (US-01.1.3, FT-01.1). Anaesthetist gains a required startDateISO; seeded anaesthetists start before the horizon, so the golden fixture holds. No Slot exists before a start date, or from today for an inactive anaesthetist, on any path (seed, Add, Edit, roll-forward, horizon extension). Add generates from the later of the start date and the demo clock's today; editing the date or the active flag adds or removes Slots from today on and refuses when a removed Slot holds a List.
- The Admin Day grid's roster comes from rosterForDate (the anaesthetist master, every anaesthetist with a Slot that day), not the static cast array, so added anaesthetists appear (US-01.4.2). The Day view's date picker is bounded by the horizon, with a "Beyond the schedule horizon" state.
- Delete listForSlot rather than re-implementing it, so the compiler shows every caller (including the Phase 14 registry entries stage-post-op and ingest-pdf-row). Each caller must handle "no List in this Slot": integrations park instead of silently retiming, the post-op addendum creates its List in today's open session (interim until 38b), the PDF picker and the Move Booking picker offer Lists only.
- Session 1 must end green and demoable: the drawer, phone advice, Edit list, Reassign and Add anaesthetist get the minimal re-point the compiler forces (work item 12, "compile-forced minimums"), and session 2 finishes them. Roll-forward and addAnaesthetist read the seed's SEED_LEAVE once masters.availability is gone.
- Assignment needs a surgeon and a hospital and is an office action (US-01.3.3); a closed hospital is flagged on the new List, never blocked. Assigning onto an unavailable or holiday session is refused for now and labelled interim (Phase 30 makes it accept-and-flag). The surgeon picker is 17's SurgeonPicker (not-preferred surgeons in their own group), a not-preferred choice shows 17's NotPreferredWarning with "Assign anyway", and assignListToSlot writes 17's list.pairingAcknowledged for an office actor through notPreferredEntryIds: warn, never block (US-01.3.5, OQ-43). Phone advice now assigns first, so its acknowledgement moves from onBookingCreated to the assignment, written once. Add store/slotActions.ts to officePrivacy.test.ts's allowlist.
- A Slot holding a List that the anaesthetist marks unavailable keeps its List with a conflict flag, labelled interim: Phase 32 builds return to the office or assign to a colleague (US-01.5.5).
- Reassign moves the List between Slots: same id, Bookings and history; target session open; the vacated session returns to Free by default (OQ-84's recommendation, provisional in the Decisions log), with Unavailable or Holiday on request. No absorb, no regenerate. Candidates are open sessions (isOpenSlot) still shown through 17's AnaesthetistCandidates (groups, tier order, shuffle, filter, Preferred pills). Keep 17's acknowledgement for an office move onto a not-preferred pairing, in reassignList (the office entry point), not in moveListToSlot, which Phases 32 and 32a reuse for the anaesthetist's own moves and which must read, check and log no preference (US-01.4.5). A move recalculates no prepayment and no invoice (US-06.3.5, US-06.5.4: an honour system); if any re-check sits on reassignList or reassignBooking, remove it with its tests and log it. Mark moveListToSlot as the seam for Phase 41's payee repoint (OQ-80) and build nothing for it.
- The office availability finder (work item 20; US-01.4.2, US-01.3.5, US-01.3.6): "Find available" in the Admin Day header opens a sheet listing everyone with an open session that day and session, through 17's rankAnaesthetistCandidates and AnaesthetistCandidates only (tier order, shuffled within a tier from a date, session and suggestionSeq key, the tier filter, and with a surgeon chosen the not-preferred group apart); picking a row opens Assign List with that surgeon. Admin only: the mobile and web finders stay name-ordered with no tier or preference, and 17's privacy test stays green.
- Default AM and PM times live in the schedule-settings record; per-Slot overrides live on the Slot and travel with a reassigned List. Remove the four hardcoded duplicates. A session keeps its default shape on the grid even with one Booking (US-01.1.4's note).
- Empty Slots carry no List state. Every List created by assignment or projection is ACTIVE (15b's rename); print states as 15b does (ACTIVE, SUBMITTED, AUTHORISED), with no "Open" label; DRAFT and "Draft List" stay free for Phase 31.
- Use the catalogue's words in new copy: "reassign" or "move", not "swap"; "recurring booking" where the catalogue says it (the Permanent List screens are renamed in Phase 30, not here).
- Stay in scope: no Draft Lists, pairing enforcement, availability calendar or status editor, conflict clearing or colour change, return-or-assign, anaesthetist move flow or update email.
- One demo trigger: the PWA-only, badged office stand-in "Office assigns a List to my next free session" on Mobile Lists, with its body in src/store/officeStandIn.ts (acting as OFFICE_SIMULATION_ACTOR, "today" from the demo clock, scanning to the horizon end) and its entry in the shared registry; its three fixed choices are pinned by a seed test as never a not-preferred pairing with Dr Souter, and its body reads no preference (it is in the PWA closure). Nothing is added to the harness bar or the Control Panel page, because every office action in this phase, the horizon change included, is demoable through the Admin app in the framed build.
- Design: teal the only action colour, crimson unused on the new sheets, Admin sheets through useSurface().Overlay, and no en or em dashes in any app copy.

- Mock backend only. Every write goes through a store action and the audited mutate(), with before/after metas and demo-clock timestamps. Components never own domain state. setAvailabilityRange, clearAvailability, createAvailabilitySeries and deleteAvailabilitySeries are each one mutate(), all or nothing on refusal, with one audit entry plus one entry per List affected.
- One mechanism (OQ-27 and OQ-64 answered): the calendar writes the Slot's own status directly. A series is a rule (weekdays, sessions, every 1 to 4 weeks, start, optional until) plus exceptions; it writes its Slots, and a single-instance edit or delete is stored as an exception so no later series write, clock roll forward or horizon extend overwrites it. Nothing reads availability from anywhere but Slots. A series fills only Slots at the default status and in no other series, and reports the rest as left as they are. One helper, seriesCalendarFor, feeds active series (with their exceptions) into 28's generateCanvasForDates as calendar input on every generating path (rollCanvasForward, setHorizonMonths when extending, an earlier start date or a reactivation), so they are painted with the anaesthetist's calendar BEFORE recurring bookings (OQ-81 part 1, settled). A recurring booking on a series-closed session is then never placed: 28's generator returns it as a RecurringClash, which Phase 31 turns into a Draft List. The generator's closed test reads isClosed over the status records; it still draws no RNG. Series rules live in src/domain/availabilitySeries.ts, pure and tested.
- The Slot status set becomes a user-maintained master list (masters.slotStatuses, US-01.2.2), availability values only. Each status has a fixed internal ID (SlotStatusKey) that rules read, never its label; seeded IDs are 28's stored values (free, holiday, unavailable), and an added status gets a generated ST#### ID, shown read only and never edited. Label, long label, description, colour (one of the six design tokens) and treatment are editable, plus "open for bookings" and "anaesthetists can set". Seed the owner's starting set: Free (default, dashed), Holiday (long label "On holiday") and Unavailable (hatched), with today's colours. No "available for emergency" and no extra kinds of unavailable seeded; an admin can add them (Greg: several kinds of unavailable, same effect). A rename or recolour shows in every view with every rule unchanged, and an added status works with no code change (US-01.2.2's two criteria; test both). No provisional line anywhere.

- Availability is not the List's state (US-01.2.2 since 2026-10-07): the status master, the display key, the legends and the filters never hold DRAFT, ACTIVE, SUBMITTED or AUTHORISED. ListState and its chip stay on the List, untouched. validateSlotStatusDraft refuses a label or long label that is a List state or its label (plus "Draft" and "Draft List", ready for Phase 31), the Availability statuses header says this is a session's availability, not a List's state (Draft, Active, Submitted, Authorised), and the table marks rows "In use" or "Retired", never "Active". Log the guard on the "For the owner's review" list.

The clash comes from one pure function, availabilityClash: a bookable status clears that List's availability conflict and raises nothing; a closed status over a List flags it once, replacing rather than stacking, with the List's surgeon, hospital, Bookings, times and List type untouched. That flag is the interim for one settled rule, Phase 32's return-or-assign (US-01.5.5) when an anaesthetist marks a session holding a List unavailable; its doc comment says so, and says generation never reaches it (a recurring booking on a closed session is traced as 28's clash for Phase 31's Draft List, not placed and flagged). - A conflict is only what the catalogue keeps at 60e2d1e (unchanged since 3d3a18c): a List at a closed hospital, or a List (and its Bookings) in a session already marked unavailable. A recurring booking landing on an unavailable session is not a conflict: the projection and generator skip it and report it as skipped, and Phase 31 turns it into a Draft List. An anaesthetist marking a booked session unavailable is return-or-assign (US-01.5.5, Phase 32): build no prompt and no Draft List conversion here; until 32 it is flagged as a named interim, through 29's per-Slot write helper, the one place 32 hooks in. - A conflict clears when its clash goes (holiday deleted or moved, hospital changed, Slot marked available, List reassigned to an available anaesthetist) or when the office clears it. An office clear holds only while its cause stands, needs no reason, and can be undone with "Flag again".

While working:
- Invoke the /frontend-design:frontend-design skill before building or reshaping any UI (screens, sheets, dialogs, panels, rows, banners, pills, empty and error states), and apply it inside this repo's design system: the docs/design files and src/theme tokens stay authoritative (convention 17, ROADMAP.md "Front-end design"), so use the skill for layout, hierarchy, spacing, states and finish, never for a new palette, typeface or visual language. Store, seed and pure-domain steps do not need it.
- Mock backend only. Every write goes through a store action and the audited mutate(), with before and after metas and timestamps from the demo clock. Components never own domain state. The writes are create, assign, re-date and remove for Draft Lists, the recurring clash, Bookings added to and moved off them through the existing Booking actions, and the pairing refusals.
- The DRAFT state. ListState becomes 'DRAFT' | 'ACTIVE' | 'SUBMITTED' | 'AUTHORISED', and List is a union on it: AssignedList (ACTIVE, SUBMITTED or AUTHORISED, with an anaesthetist and a Slot) and DraftList (DRAFT, no anaesthetist, no Slot, a draft trail). LIST_STATE_LABELS gains DRAFT, shown only on a Draft List beside "Unassigned"; no assigned List reads "DRAFT" or "Open". Only an ACTIVE List can be completed and submitted; submitting or authorising a Draft List refuses with "Assign an anaesthetist first.". A store-wide invariant test holds FT-07.1: every ACTIVE, SUBMITTED or AUTHORISED List has its anaesthetist, Slot, day, session, hospital and surgeon, and every DRAFT List has neither anaesthetist nor Slot.
- A Draft List is a List record, plus a draft trail (origin, source, since, by, and for a recurring clash the day it stands for). There is no separate collection, so its Bookings hang off its List id and assignment keeps the id.
  - createDraftList and the recurring clash write through one insertDraftList and leave schedule.slots and every other List untouched; prove it with a test.
  - Only assignDraftList takes DRAFT to ACTIVE, through attachListToSlot, in the session its day and session fix (the admin chooses only the anaesthetist), in one commit with a draftList.assign meta (before state DRAFT, after ACTIVE); the draft trail is kept with assignedAtISO. 15a's warnings are derived on read (store/warnings.ts), so they need no call, but their rules and OpenWarningRow must tolerate a Booking on a Draft List.
  - An ACTIVE List only becomes a Draft List through detachListToDraft, built and unit-tested here for Phase 32 (origins movedToOffice and unavailableReturn); it refuses a SUBMITTED or AUTHORISED List, and no screen in this phase calls it.
  - removeDraftList soft-removes and refuses while it holds an active Booking; redateDraftList moves it and its Bookings to another day and keeps its waiting time.
  - A Draft List cannot be submitted, authorised or billed, and an anaesthetist cannot add a Booking to one.
  - No projection, roll forward or new-anaesthetist path may overwrite a List id a Draft List holds, and a recurring booking never projects a second List or Draft List for a day it already answered for (the Draft List keeps recurringBookingId, and a recurring clash keeps the day in draft.projectedFor even after a re-date or a removal).
- Prepayment (US-06.3.5, US-06.5.4, D20). A Booking on a Draft List has no anaesthetist, so 27's routine generates and withdraws nothing for it. Built default, logged for the owner: the first assignment of a Booking that never had an anaesthetist is its setup, so assignDraftList runs 27's routine (syncPrepayment) once for it with the listAssigned cause 27's plan exports for this call (use the name 27 shipped); reassigning a List that already had an anaesthetist (one Phase 32 returned), re-dating and detaching re-trigger nothing (honour system; the payee repoint is Phase 41's, D38).
- The recurring clash (OQ-81 part 2, built as the answer): one pure recurringClashDraft turns each clash (Phase 28's generator RecurringClash or Phase 30's skipped entry: a recurring booking covering a closed session) into a DRAFT Draft List with the recurring booking's hospital, surgeon, kind and notes, origin recurringClash and a stable derived id. It runs on every path that yields clashes: addRecurringBooking, editRecurringBooking, applyRecurringBookingsToCanvas ("Apply to canvas now"), every roll forward, the horizon extend, an earlier start date, and the seed's generation. Confirm 29 paints series onto new Slots before recurring bookings (seriesCalendarFor in the generator's calendar step), so no recurring booking is ever left flagged on the anaesthetist; if any path still places and then flags, fix it (work item 9). Never for a past day. It writes no availability conflict and never shows on Conflicts for the anaesthetist (US-01.5.4 AC3); a hospital closure still raises Phase 30's holiday conflict. An edit, retire or move of the recurring booking updates or removes an empty clash Draft List and keeps one with Bookings. Phase 30's sheet wording for skipped sessions now says they go to Draft Lists.
- Phase 17's helper, privately (US-01.3.5, US-01.3.6, D44). The assign picker is draftListAvailability (free, not available, occupied; no preference knowledge) ranked once by 17's rankAnaesthetistCandidates and split into Free, "Not preferred with Ms Reid", Not available and Already has a List (disabled), each tier-ordered and shuffled within a tier (requestKey from the List id and suggestionSeq), with 17's tier filter, Preferred pills and an Everyone / Free only toggle. assignDraftList writes list.pairingAcknowledged (through notPreferredEntryIds) only for a not-preferred pairing. Add store/draftListActions.ts to the allowlist in 17's officePrivacy.test.ts as an office write path; it must not import store/officePrivate.ts, so the PWA closure check stays green. The PWA stand-in skips a not-preferred pairing through draftListFitsAnaesthetist, a boolean, and never names a preference or a tier. No inline preference checks or sorts anywhere.
- Warn, never block, except for the real guards:
  - A closed session (Unavailable, Holiday or any closed status) or a not-preferred choice is selectable, warned before save, and assigned. A closed session gets a conflict through Phase 30's path and its warning tells the office to check with the anaesthetist (US-01.4.7); a not-preferred pairing gets 17's acknowledgement entry.
  - Only these refuse: an occupied session, a missing hospital, surgeon, day or session, a passed day, removing a Draft List with active Bookings, detaching a List that is not ACTIVE, and the office-only guard.
  - A Draft List at a hospital with a holiday carries Phase 30's holiday conflict like any List, and shows on the Conflicts screen as "Unassigned".

Phase goal: a List with no anaesthetist is a Draft List, the first state (DRAFT) of a List's lifecycle. Each has a hospital, surgeon, day and session, all required, may already hold Bookings, and takes no Slot. Draft Lists are flagged "Unassigned" with their waiting time and sorted soonest first, beside the day in the right rail, in a band on the Day dashboard, on their own Admin page and on a nav badge. Only the office assigns one, by choosing the anaesthetist from everyone potentially available, grouped and tier-ordered by Phase 17's helper with soft warnings and a full audit, and it becomes ACTIVE; or the office removes it or changes its date. A recurring booking that lands on a session the anaesthetist has already marked unavailable becomes a Draft List. Every List has exactly one hospital and one surgeon, and the Day dashboard shows each block's booking count.
32Anaesthetist moves their own List, and the notification poolDone 15 October 2026

An anaesthetist returns their own List to the office (it goes back to DRAFT) or moves it into a colleague's free session, at once, with no preference warning, also when they mark a booked session unavailable.

After 29 and 31. 2 sessions. Schedule track. Questions: OQ-79, OQ-84.

- The receiver rule (US-01.4.7, OQ-85 answered, D61): the receiver is found by the list of colleagues free in that session or by type-ahead; only an active colleague whose Slot on the Booking's day and session is available (isOpenForBooking) and holds no List is offered, sorted by name only, never grouped or ordered by a preference or a tier (US-01.4.7's criterion, OQ-113, US-01.4.5); a colleague on leave, unavailable or already holding a List shows when searched for, as a neutral row that cannot be picked, and the store refuses them too. A colleague on leave is offered only after they set that session available themselves, which is their acceptance; no trigger or store path ever sets anyone available.

A drafted action may touch only the snapshot's slices and never emit an app event (the emitter is shared by every store); a demo Reset discards a dirty draft. - One save is one change set: one change-set id across the entries, a booking.save header, the office as the actor. The lastModified stamps never appear in audit before or after. A save applies the admin's own changes onto the real Booking as it is at Save time, reading each before value from it; a cancel, move or authorise in between blocks it with a plain reason. No row versions, merge or clash prompt: concurrent edits (US-02.5.6) are Future Work. Remap draft-created ids against the real counters and re-check the existing invariants through their own guards (22's resolveSplit, one primary Procedure, 20's contractCandidatesFor, 24's price-precedence rejections, the source texts only appended, validateNhi). A blank procedure or a missing Contract never refuses a save (D33). A save that changed the Procedures, a Contract or the payer runs 27's re-check once on the real store (one prepayment invoice with its ledger pair and draft Xero pair, never two); in the draft store it is a no-op; a move never re-checks (D20).
- The text as received is never edited (US-02.5.7, 20a). On an amend the office adds a description beside it through 20a's Add a description (Told by Phone or Email), which in draft mode is a drafted append applied at Save by 20a's append rules; the invoice description is a drafted edit applied through setInvoiceDescription (a blank refused); a draft that changed an existing text refuses; editProcedure is never handed sourceTexts.
- Every List owner change (28's reassignment, 31's assignment, 32's moves, whichever app makes them) carries a listReassign change set and shows in the History of the List's Bookings; a List reassignment is its own event, not a save, and its flow keeps no email step (US-01.4.1).
- The office can edit every field of a Booking (US-02.3.1's note): add the NHI to the office's Edit patient sheet (validateNhi; mandatory and never cleared, D11: an empty value is refused, no blank or pending state), let the office correct the RVG code, the Contract and the invoice description until AUTHORISED (on a SUBMITTED List as on an ACTIVE one or a Draft List), give the time a proper field, and open up any other field the drift check found or record why not. AUTHORISED stays read-only (25's snapshot); 19b's locked age and P1 modifiers stay locked.
- Keep the change-set maths, the as-at reconstruction, the template validation and rendering, and the email builder and picker pure, in src/domain, with Vitest tests. Order as-at by audit sequence, not time (the demo clock has minute resolution).
- The email is a real mailto link, on demand only: a Draft update email button on every Admin Booking and in the List drawer, and Draft cover-change email on a List-move notification in 32's pool; never a prompt after a save, a reassignment or an assignment, and never sent automatically for any change, the office's or an anaesthetist's (OQ-82 answered: manual in the first release; US-02.3.5 Future). The admin picks one or more rows from the history (saves, creations, cancellations, moves, applied hospital rows, coalesced anaesthetist edits, cover changes); only those appear, netted per field. It starts from the admin-maintained template for the kind of change (Change of anaesthetist, Cancellation, Correction of an error, Booking change), editable in Master data. Opening the mail client writes one "Update email drafted (not sent)" entry and nothing else; nothing is stored or labelled as sent. The whole encoded link is at most 2,000 characters (one labelled constant), and a shortened body says so. Only the allowlisted fields appear (a Procedure named by its RVG group's description, else "Procedure 2"): never an NHI, date of birth, internal notes, the text as received, the invoice description, the RVG code, Contract or holder, payer or insurance indication, modifiers and explanations, money, or pairing preferences and tiers (17's office-private slice), and a List email names no patient. A single-Booking move (32a, if DONE) is a Booking change for the rooms with the new anaesthetist on its line, not a cover change (OQ-46: a new anaesthetist on a List); its email is optional for the mover and the office, and its pool notice optional too (D61, D58); 32a's handoff reads it as a cover change, so log the reading for the owner. A List returned to the office is not a cover change until the Draft List is assigned (its row is shown but not emailable; picked with the later assignment it nets to one line). The anaesthetist's typed move message (32's Notification.message) is shown to the admin in the composer as context, never put into the email body; log both readings.

- US-08.3.1 (Contradicts): the pair is made with the invoice, in the same mutate(), never at the handoff, so a handoff fault leaves a complete pair marked "Not yet in Xero" (re-point "Arm handoff failure"); a procedure pair's payable is to the anaesthetist, a prepayment's to the AA Holding account; a reversal (credit) is Phase 39's, on signed leg entries. Never re-derive it from the List; delete both copies of anaesthetistIdForCase. A payee never changes: no repointPayable exists (D38), and a moved prepaid Booking changes nothing.
- Every Booking pair carries the patient's hidden id whoever pays (the patient, the payer on the Booking such as a guardian, an insurer, a hospital or another Contract holder), so the receivable reaches its patient and Phase 40's balance can be patient-centric (US-13.2.2, DM-23). patientLedgerPosition covers every payer, with the billable party and invoice date on each row and a signed outstandingTotal; it has no screen here.
- Every invoice creator makes its pair in the same mutate() that raises the invoice: the billing run, the retry, the prepayment generation (admin approval, or at once when approval is switched off; the one creation point, OQ-80 and D38, where Phase 27 already writes the invoice, its case and the draft ACCREC and DRAFT ACCPAY, so US-09.1.3 must not regress; before generation there is no invoice and no pair, so no held or rewritten pair), 27a's authorise hook (a LedgerTransfer, the transfer from the AA Holding account to the anaesthetist who did the List: two payable legs, never a receivable, with 27a's Xero journal; neither money in nor out of AA) and the AA fee run (inside raiseAnaesthetistInvoiceInto). No move, List or Booking, before or after authorise, changes any pair (nothing detects or re-checks a move). There is no prepaid excess and no balance invoice: a prepaid Procedure is priced at the prepaid amount and nothing is raised automatically afterwards (US-06.4.1, OQ-61), and prepaid Procedures are left off the final invoice with nothing deducted (D57), so add no excess or deduction field anywhere. The Xero handoff only reads the legs and stamps mirror ids. A handoff fault leaves a complete pair marked "Not yet in Xero".
- Phase 16's bctiRecords re-reads from the payable legs (bctiRecordsOf), never state.xero.accPays, with receivablePaidAtISO from the receivable leg's paidInAtISO; bctisFor and its paidOnly switch stay the only count, with a parity test; prepayment and settlement pairs stay outside the count by 27a's default (prepaymentPairs: false, one place, owner's list).
- Both legs move together. A receipt updates received and released in one commit, through Phase 16's payableReleasedFor, never a second copy. Nothing disburses above released, and cumulative leg amounts always equal the sums of their entries.
- The ledger maths is pure, in src/domain/billing/ledger.ts, with Vitest tests. The imbalance is receipts held less payables due, exact to the cent. AA fee money (AA's own, never netted against payables) is never part of it; the AA Holding account's balance is subtracted (imbalance = receipts held - payables due - holding balance), and a transfer moves neither received nor disbursed. The per-anaesthetist positions plus the unattributed held receipts sum to the whole ledger, anaesthetistsOutOfBalance lists exactly the anaesthetists whose scoped imbalance is non-zero or who have a check, and the seed is in balance with that list empty.
- No app money view (web, mobile, admin, shared) reads state.xero. Only the Xero simulation and the webhook picker do (plus MasterData's Xero archiving view, not a money view); extend src/apps/moneyViewPurity.test.ts to admin and shared with those reads as a named allow-list, and reword its "mirror" comment. payablesDue and the payables run work from the ledger alone, stay an on-demand office action here (OQ-47 Proposed: the weekly ISO-week cycle, US-10.2.7, is Phase 39a's) and keep byAnaesthetist a signed per-payee sum; payablesDue is the one source 39a's weekly run, netting and period approval read, so add no second payables selector and no week or cycle copy. Fee payments never reach GST activity, payment history or the BCTI count.
- A billing-run failure becomes a BillingException, not a ledger pair, one per Booking: the Booking fails whole and the List never fails (OQ-05). An unmatched receipt is held in the ledger, and only the office clears it, by allocating or refunding it. A receipt whose bank reference names an anaesthetist carries that anaesthetistId and puts them out of balance until cleared; one naming no one counts only in the whole ledger.
- Leg entries accept signed amounts for Phases 39 and 39a (OQ-42), but add no credit or netting path. Nothing is built for a negative with no later payment (OQ-71, D21: settled outside the system). Receipts and disbursements stay audited through mutate(); the catalogue's audit scope (DM-45) is Phase 39's call. Receipts held includes prepayments received, which sit in the AA Holding account (27a) until the List is authorised; refunds from it are Phase 41's.

While working:
- Invoke the /frontend-design:frontend-design skill before building or reshaping any UI (screens, sheets, dialogs, panels, rows, banners, pills, empty and error states), and apply it inside this repo's design system: the docs/design files and src/theme tokens stay authoritative (convention 17, ROADMAP.md "Front-end design"), so use the skill for layout, hierarchy, spacing, states and finish, never for a new palette, typeface or visual language. Store, seed and pure-domain steps do not need it. Here it applies to the events list (anaesthetist and office variants, empty state), the Review Events tab, the review sheet, the waiting-for-the-run panel, the button on the stack's Contract row beside Split, the additional-invoice form in its three wrappers (the mobile bottom sheet is mobile-first, not a modal), the invoice document's link line and rail card and the Invoices Kind column.
- Mock backend only. Every write is a store action through the audited mutate(), with before and after metas and timestamps from the demo clock. Components never own domain state. No Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random().
- One element, thin: one ProcedureEvent record in schedule.procedureEvents, one store action per step (add, update, remove, approve, decline, run), the kind's rules only in EVENT_KINDS, status derived by eventStatus and never stored, and nothing kind-specific outside the detail union and additionalInvoice.ts, so Phase 39 (credits) and 39b (pre-op and post-op) add kinds without reshaping it. No modifier, ASA, base-unit, RVG or Contract field on the record.
- One standard review step: every billable event, whoever raised it, is approved (or declined, with a reason) by the office in Admin Review's Events tab; approval raises nothing. Only runEventInvoicing raises an event's invoice, exactly once, in the next billing run; runBillingForList is not changed, never sweeps events and never creates one, so the S3 and S5 invoice numbers never shift and nothing is raised automatically after a prepaid or fixed-price procedure. eventInvoicing decides "bundled on the run's invoice for the same billable party" or "own invoice", in the next run, never held for the Procedure's invoice; the additional invoice is always its own, so only that branch is reachable here (39b's kinds reach the first).
- The additional invoice: a button on the Procedure's Contract row (20a's stack, beside 22's Split), "Create additional invoice" in Admin on any invoiced Procedure and "Raise additional invoice" on mobile and web only on the anaesthetist's own (procedureOwner, the locked payee), reached through 38a's search. One shared, PWA-safe AdditionalInvoiceForm: free form (description x quantity x amount; the pricing module imports no fee, Contract, line, resolver, precedence, unit, modifier or RVG code), the "Same as the original invoice" tick ticked by default, unticking to 21's party search for any billable party with its invoice email (audited), recorded as an event and reviewed, then raised by the run through 22's materialiseInvoices (layout, delivery and GST from the original invoice, never its prices) with its own AA-2026- number, the additionalTo lineage, eventId, its own ledger pair through newBookingPair (payable equal to receivable, to the original's payee), its own Xero pair through handoffPair with no NHI or patient name, and exactly one BCTI record through bctiRecordsOf (counted by 16's bctisFor under its paid-only rule like any other, so nothing until it is paid). On a prepaid Procedure the original is its prepayment invoice. Links both ways are derived selectors.
- Immutability: the original Booking, its Procedures, its lock and the original invoice are never written (US-07.3.2), and 38a's read-only rule for an invoiced List gains only this button; assert deep-equal in the tests.
- The events list: one shared component under each Procedure in BookingDetailBody for mobile, web and Admin. On their own Procedure the anaesthetist sees kind, date, description, who raised it, status, total ex GST and party, and Edit and Remove on what they raised until it is approved; never a review action, invoice link or Booking total; they see the section only when the Procedure has an event. The office sees all of it with the invoice link and the actions. Every word comes from src/shared/procedureEventCopy.ts, the one place for the label "event" and both button labels.
- The Booking total (US-03.1.10, Admin only): one selector, bookingTotalWithEvents, feeds the total, each Procedure's price (24's price function) and its events; every invoice-ticked event counts, ex GST with GST beside (our reading, logged); prices change only through 24's PriceOverrideSheet from a Change price control; nothing is written to the Booking; the anaesthetist sees no Booking total.
- Remove, do not hide, the addendum Booking: addPostOpAddendum, its test and the addendum cases in store/bookingSource.test.ts, bookingType, addendumOfBookingId, the banner, "Add post-op event", the addendum source mapping, 20's addendum Contract copy and 28's addendum List helper. Nothing replaces the block: the anaesthetist's route is Raise additional invoice; 39b adds its event control beside it.
- Seed: one additionalInvoice event awaiting review on Dr Souter's oa03 Procedure (David Chen, one line "Post-op ward review, Tue 23 Jun, 20 min" at $85.00, same-as party), raised by Dr Souter, RNG-free, at a fixed instant, raising no invoice, so no seeded figure moves; pin it in seed.test.ts. Bump PERSIST_VERSION (once per session that changes shape or seed, or once for the phase) with persistMigrate tests, including a stale payload with an addendum Booking.
- Demo triggers go in Phase 14's registry only, never on the Control Panel page: re-point stage-post-op (authorise and bill only, new copy), add run-next-billing-run (Admin · Billing monitor and Admin · Review, bar; body in src/store; disabled when nothing is waiting), add the PWA-only office-reviews-additional-invoice on the mobile Booking detail (approve as OFFICE_SIMULATION_ACTOR, then a run narrowed to that event), and route regenerate-invoice for an additional invoice. Create additional invoice, Raise additional invoice, Approve for billing and Decline are product actions.
- Words: Booking, not Card; never "slot" in app copy; never "timesheet"; no "DRAFT" for an assigned List; no en or em dashes in any app copy. Teal is the only action colour; status pills on semantic tints; mono tabular numbers for dates, numbers and amounts; no crimson on controls.
- Keep npm run build, npm run build:pwa and npx vitest run green as you go, and re-green at the session 1 stop point before starting session 2. Add Vitest tests for every pure rule, refusal and action (the office and the owning anaesthetist, a colleague refused), the run's idempotence and fault path, the "After a fixed price" case, the BCTI count, the seed pin, the triggers and the acceptance criteria.

- Mock backend only, through store actions and the audited mutate(). The original Booking, its Procedures, its lock and pricing snapshots and the original invoice are never written: "Credit waiting for review", "Credited" and every link are derived selectors.
- A credit is an event on 38b's element (a new kind, its label in 38b's one label place; EVENT_KINDS.credit with addedBy office and anaesthetist, anaesthetistOwnOnly, reviewed). The credit event and its replacements (38b additional invoices) are submitted, reviewed and issued as one correction: one row in 38b's review queue (naming who raised it), no second queue, and issued together inside 38b's runEventInvoicing commit (its generic loop skips replacements; its handoff loop sends a correction to handoffCorrection, never handoffPair). liveInvoiceForProcedure skips a credited invoice and returns its live replacement. Compute the reversal plan from the legs at issue time. Nothing but a person's action raises a credit (US-06.4.1).
- Who may credit: the office on any Procedure; the anaesthetist on their own Procedure only, through 38b's mayRaise and procedureOwner, enforced in the store (notOwnProcedure) as well as the UI, reached through 38a's search. The anaesthetist's form shows the original's number, party and total and "Your payment for this invoice is reversed.", lets them add new invoices, and has no split, no copy and no ledger figures. Its office review on the PWA is the PWA-only stand-in "Office reviews this credit" (office-reviews-credit, body in src/store), following 38b's office-reviews-additional-invoice, which must skip replacement events.
- The credit note option credits the original in full to any billable party (default the original's, the choice audited), with a reason, and zero or more replacements; "Credit in full and rebill" (the office's) opens with one replacement to the original's party, which from session 2 holds a copy of the original's lines and holder references (US-08.6.6; each line at quantity 1 for its stored ex-GST amount, any negative line folded into the line it follows, so the copy totals the original exactly and passes priceFreeFormInvoice). A rebill's total is whatever the correction needs: the equal-total rule is the split's only. A credit note is not a refund: no money moves and nothing pays a held credit out. A rebill never re-prices from a Contract, never rewrites the Booking's lock and pays the locked payee.
- On issue, one engine commit: the credit note, the receivable credit, a negative invoice to the anaesthetist on every credit (the payable is always reversed: the undisbursed part offset, the paid-out part left as its recoveryDue for 39a to net in the weekly run), and the replacements through 38b's issue path with the rebillOf lineage; then one Xero commit (reversal and every new pair together, with the fault path and an idempotent retry; 38b's own handoff never runs twice for a replacement). Payments on a credited ACCREC, payables over a reversed ACCPAY (through 37's payBillsInXero too, if 37 ran; narrate the Stage refund after payout as paid in Xero, 37's Run payables in Xero) and a late handoff of a credited invoice are refused. runPayables does not net the negative invoice. No Xero credit note carries an NHI, a patient name or other personal information (OQ-30).
- The split (US-08.6.4) is the same flow with cause 'split', office only, only once the Procedure's invoice exists (before that it is a normal admin edit, so the action is disabled with "Not invoiced yet. Edit the Booking's Procedures, Contracts and prices instead."): at least two blank component cards the office adds to (nothing to prefill from, and no combination test), each with its own party and a freely typed amount, which must add up to the credited invoice ex GST to the cent (splitTotalCheck, pure, in the store too; per-invoice GST rounding accepted and shown), with a live Left to allocate figure and Submit disabled until it is $0.00. A prepaid combination's split, and any credit on a prepayment invoice, stay refused (they need Phase 41's credit of a prepayment invoice): record them as known gaps against US-08.6.4 and US-08.6.5 and hand them to 41.

Time from the demo clock only; no Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random().

Payments, receipts and disbursements are kept out of the audit viewer and entity History by one pure exclusion list (AUDIT_VIEWER_EXCLUDED, tested); the store's record keeps them.

The ledger (Phase 36) stays the system of record and the engine never performs a disbursement: the week's schedule is paid in Xero (the accountant, simulated), and the engine records each bill payment (37's recordDisbursement) and each credit allocation (the new recordOffset) only when Xero reports it, by webhook, poll or the outage queue, idempotent by key.
- The weekly cycle is US-10.2.7: one run per ISO week, named "Week 30"; the office closes the week (the snapshot of released, unpaid payables, the ledger at close, and the reconciliation against the simulated bank download), works the Monday anomalies (hold out of this run, rolling the line into the next week tagged "Rolled from Week 30", or mark fixed), approves the week's BCTIs, and sends the payment schedule to the accountant. A payable released after the close belongs to the next week. Every payment shows the week of the run it was paid in. The demo clock does not gate the steps (it is fixed on Tue 21 Jul, ISO week 30, and forward-only): each step works in order on any day, shows its planned date from the setting and stamps the clock's actual time; the cycle never advances the clock. Log that as a built default for the owner.
- The approval gate is the point of US-10.2.6: no path pays a payable that is not an unheld line of an approved, scheduled run (runPayables, disbursePayable, the Xero-side bill payment, the Xero pair trigger, a replay, the poll, the queue). Approval refuses while a Monday anomaly is open. The one-click demo payouts approve first so the gate has no back door: the Xero sim's "Simulate payment and payout" (S3 Beat 3) and the payout half of 39's "Stage refund after payout" go through approveAndPayPairForDemo (a one-pair staged run beside the week's run, never doubling with it), and 36's PWA "Office runs payables" becomes "Office runs this week's payment cycle" (no "payables" in the label: 14's demoTriggers.test.ts rule, as 36 narrowed it to bar entries with a companion assertion that the only "payables" label is this PWA stand-in; update that companion assertion to the label set as shipped, and keep the bar rule passing as 37 left it with its "Pay anaesthetist in Xero (payables run)" entry). A run is frozen at approval; a stale run is superseded by a Re-close, never edited.
- Netting is pure billing maths in src/domain/billing/payablesRun.ts, and the week, the cycle's days, the reconciliation and the anomalies in src/domain/billing/paymentCycle.ts, both with Vitest worked examples, including US-10.2.5's 19 positives and one negative and its remittance advice criterion, US-10.2.7's held line rolling into the next week, and ISO weeks at year ends (2026 has 53). Netting uses each negative invoice's recoveryDue (what 39 left open), never its offsetAmount, and only unheld lines. Net per anaesthetist is never below zero; what a run cannot net stays open on the negative invoice and nets in a later week with positives. An anaesthetist with only negatives is not in a run. The offset is its own amount on the payable leg (offsetAmount), never counted as cash, and the whole ledger stays in balance after the close, approval, sending and detection.
- OQ-71 (answered): a negative with no later payment is handled outside the system. The only surface is the open amount on the anaesthetist's ledger position with one caption ("If no later payment comes, AA settles it with the anaesthetist outside the system") and the web still-to-net panel. No carry-forward, recovery or write-off wording or record; "Rolled from" and "Rolls into" are only for held lines.
- runExclusionFor is the only standing exclusion point. Its first reason is 'heldInHoldingAccount', from 27a's isHeldPayable (a prepaid Booking's collect payable to the AA Holding account never enters a run; the payable the transfer makes to the anaesthetist is an ordinary line once released). 26's bank details are display-only, so a missing account is a Monday anomaly the office holds out or marks fixed, never an automatic exclusion; if 37 shipped a heldForBank refusal, it becomes a further reason. Phase 41 adds nothing here. A Monday hold is a per-week decision on the run's line, not an exclusion. Netting reads every open negative whatever its credit note's cause and whoever raised it.
- The 20th-of-the-month payment is not part of this cycle (Greg: the separate monthly non-trust cycle, Phase 16's AA fee run); build nothing for it and put it on the owner's review list.
- Time from the demo clock only, date-fns over given dates for ISO weeks; no Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random(). The staging bodies are deterministic.
- Seeded disbursements become backdrop weekly runs (one per ISO week, closed, approved and scheduled before paid) and every seeded receipt gets its bank line, with no figure moving: assert Dr Souter's position, the whole ledger, the GST schedule and S3's figures are identical before and after. The payout nearest today (Dr Souter's, 15 Jul; 27a holds the seeded prepayment, so it has none on 16 Jul) falls in Week 29, so the monitor opens on Week 30 with no run yet. Bump PERSIST_VERSION once for the new slices, setting, bank lines, leg field and backdrop runs, and extend the migrate and seed tests.
- Demo triggers go through the Phase 14 registry, shown only on their screen, with disabled states and bodies in src/store or src/shared: new "Stage a payment week" on the Billing monitor (choices "19 BCTIs" and "19 BCTIs and a credit after payout", service dates before the canvas horizon start, payments through the ordinary webhook with their bank lines, the credit built by one pure credit-after-payout fixture builder over 39's pure credit functions) and "Stage a bank mismatch" (session 2); re-point Phase 39's "Stage refund after payout" (payout through a staged run, result text), Phase 37's "Pay anaesthetist in Xero (payables run)" and Phase 36's PWA stand-in. Close the week, Hold out of this run, Mark fixed, Return to run, Approve week N's BCTIs, Send payment schedule, Re-close and Run payables in Xero are product buttons, not registry entries. Nothing is added to the Control Panel page. No new PWA entry is needed (the cycle, schedule and remittance advice are in Admin and web Accounts; mobile Balances is unchanged, a built default for the owner's review list); confirm the re-pointed stand-in works in the PWA build and pwaPurity.test.ts stays green.
- Every new audit action gets an ACTION_LABELS entry and the right actor (office for the close, holds, fixes, approval and schedule; the office stand-in for staged runs; system for detection; demo for the Xero-side batch payment and the bank lines).
- Follow the design files: teal is the only action colour, crimson stays identity only, pills use semantic tokens, amounts, weeks and dates are mono with tabular-nums, negatives render "-$X.XX" through formatSignedCurrency (neutral, never red), weekday names come from the setting through one helper. No en or em dashes in any app copy.

- A billing event (addBillingEvent, validateBillingEvent, priceBillingEvent) is a free-text charge or a credit on the Procedure, with its own date, the invoice tick and the "same as" party; it is typed, prices nothing, and is allowed on a fixed-price Procedure. In the next run it is a line on the same party's invoice (a credit a negative line, left in review if it would take that invoice below $0.00) or its own invoice; a credit that cannot be bundled goes through Phase 39's credit note (not offered if 39 has not run). Phase goal: the anaesthetist, or the office on request, adds pre-op and post-op events to a current or past Procedure on mobile, web and Admin, each with its own date and time, a recorded time or a fixed fee and no modifiers, an invoice tick and a "same as" billable party; every billable event goes through one review step and is billed in the next run, never held for the Procedure's invoice, on the same billable party's invoice in that run where there is one, otherwise on its own, traceable to the Procedure; a recorded time is priced at the Procedure's Contract rate or the anaesthetist's own, and a Contract event fee replaces it; the ACC pre-op assessment is a preconfigured pre-op event under the generic rules (codes provisional); a billing event (a free-text charge or a credit) is added to a Procedure the same way and replaces the billing line, whose entity is removed with every seeded line re-expressed at its old figure. No UNIT x RATE or Contract add-on fee line is built.

While working:
- Invoke the /frontend-design:frontend-design skill before building or reshaping any UI (screens, sheets, dialogs, panels, rows, banners, pills, empty and error states), and apply it inside this repo's design system: the docs/design files and src/theme tokens stay authoritative (convention 17, ROADMAP.md "Front-end design"), so use the skill for layout, hierarchy, spacing, states and finish, never for a new palette, typeface or visual language. Store, seed and pure-domain steps do not need it.
- Mock backend only. Every write goes through a store action and the audited mutate(), with before and after metas and demo-clock timestamps. Components never own domain state; the file input only hands CSV text to a store action.
- The pricing model stays in one place. You build no pricing structure: the specs load into the RVG group, Party, Contract, price-range, specialty, funding tag and modifier types that 18, 18a, 19, 19a and 19b keep in aa-prototype/src/domain/billing (plus the seed), through their own validators and create actions. A spec maps columns and never declares a second shape or copies a rule, so a later version of the design (AR-58, AR-61) stays a contained edit there.
- Loads never go around the guards. Stage validates and reports without touching masters. Commit re-validates, then loads exactly the valid rows in one mutate(), through the same create planners and validators the manual Add uses. A rejected row never reaches master data, and nothing loads partially. A load adds and never overwrites. The same sheets after a Reset give identical records, so a test system loads again the same way.
- The load is the developer's one-off step (US-13.4.3): the loader lives only in the demo-badged Seed load view, acting as the simulated developer actor; no office view offers "Load from spreadsheet"; there is no Contract upload or export (D34, US-04.2.13 Future Work). After the load the office keeps everything by hand.
- One set of rules: every rejected-row reason is word for word the manual action's refusal, proven by a parity test. Surgeon and anaesthetist rows use 17's HPI CPN rule, keyed on the HPI CPN; a surgeon's specialties are required (18a's refusal). A Contract's AA code is its unique key: the sheet's, or 18's next code when blank.
- Figures land where the catalogue puts them: group figures on the RVG group (above 0, an optional range; a repeated code is allowed, the hidden id is allocated); a fixed price only in price ranges (a fixed price's first range on its Contracts row, later ones on the price ranges sheet; a start may be in the past; a blank cell is not set and 0 is a value, decided by 18's and 19a's validators). A Contract states no units. There is no procedure list, general procedure, Contract line or version. No default RVG Contract, hospital or insurer default, Open price Contract or second No contract · RVG is ever created; No contract · RVG and the open-price fallbacks are never loaded (18a makes the fallbacks after a load). No NZSA guide range check and no warning on loaded figures.
- Parties, Contracts and the rest load as the doc's sheets say: Parties (kind, hospital, billing name, delivery email or post), specialties, funding tags, and Contracts one per priced item (settings, pricing kind, links, specialties, funding tag, a fixed price's first range) with their later price ranges, plus 39b's event fees. An anaesthetist's price list is one prepaid Contract per item linked to them, a surgeon's arrangement one Contract per item linked to its surgeons. 18's guards refuse the rest (a prepaid Contract that bills its holder or is not a fixed price, an override on a fixed price, a billed Party with no billing details), and the sheet shows their words.
- Modifiers: AA's own rows load from a sheet and become editable and retirable; the RVG table is 19b's, generated from AR-34's files, as printed, never re-transcribed and never edited.
- Contract edits after the load (D34 answered): 18's in-place audited edit and 19a's price range; there is no staging, "New version", review dashboard or check state of your own.
- Clean cut: Solutions Plus supplies operation names only. Its units are never read, junk such as "10% discount" is excluded and cannot be approved, only AA-approved names load (names are matched to Contract invoice descriptions, since there is no procedure list; a Contract whose name is not approved is rejected), and loaded values come from the controlled spreadsheets. No Solutions Plus identifier is carried (US-13.4.3).
- Masters referenced by identity (hospitals, surgeons, rooms, groups and AA modifiers) retire, never delete. Retire needs a reason and refuses while upcoming work or an Active Contract depends on the record. Pickers hide retired records; every display keeps resolving them. No contract · RVG is never retired. Parties, Contracts, specialties and funding tags keep the lifecycle 18 and 18a gave them.
- Insurers and funders are Parties (US-11.4.1 Retired, D55): no Insurers view, direct-claims flag, pre-fill or insurer retire kind. The Parties sheet loads them with the delivery setting (email or post); practitioner links sit on the Contract; the split is a Booking action (22).
- The RVG time rule becomes editable by tier (19a's handoff, US-05.2.2); a part interval always rounds up and is shown read-only. AUTHORISED Procedures and raised invoices keep their figures.
- Recurring bookings: use Phase 30's names and copy ("recurring bookings", never "Permanent Lists") in the view, blockers and copy.
- Public holidays are one master calendar that raises conflicts through 30's derived rule, with no stamping. Every holiday reader goes through closuresFor / closuresIndex, so none loses Labour Day when the per-hospital rows go. The migration must not move the flagged-List set. Regenerate only the golden fixture's conflicts column and bump PERSIST_VERSION by one.
- Demo triggers: register "Load sample spreadsheet", and "Go-live data load (demo)" in Phase 14's registry, bar only, on Admin Master data only, as the phase doc's table says. They stage and never load by themselves. The samples carry invalid rows, among them a prepaid Contract with an RVG pricing kind and a surgeon with no specialty. Keep the bodies in src/shared and the sample fixtures in src/domain, so pwaPurity.test.ts holds. Add nothing to the Control Panel page. There is no PWA stand-in: nothing here is mobile.
- Copy: one label, "Master data", never "reference tables" or "reference data" (Greg, 2026-10-02); never "slot" (say session); a Party, never "contract holder"; no "default RVG Contract", "protected default", "New version" or Contract lines anywhere; no en or em dashes in any app copy.
- Design: one pattern across every Master data view (grouped sub-nav, the shared header, Show retired, edit sheet with Retire and Reinstate); 18's, 18a's and 19a's screens are refitted, never rebuilt; admin sheets go through useSurface().Overlay; the result panel is an Admin Review style table; teal the only action colour; every outcome pill carries its word.
- Keep npm run build, npm run build:pwa and npx vitest run green as you go. Add Vitest tests for the CSV parser, the blank-and-zero helper, validateSheet and every spec, the rule-parity test, the AR-34 table round trip, masterLifecycle and retire blockers, holidays and the conflict extension, the time-rule edit, the in-place Contract edit and price range, the load and go-live store actions (including stale re-validation, no partial load, the fallbacks after a load and the repeatable load after Reset), the Solutions Plus rules, the US-13.4.1 coverage test, the pickers, the triggers and the seed invariants.

Donald's 5 November decisions are made for the build: never Contract lines, versions or staged changes. If a rule it needs is missing or wrong, fix it in that module with its test and log it. Standing patterns are recurring bookings (Phase 30), never Permanent List templates. Assigned Lists are ACTIVE, Draft Lists DRAFT. RVG groups have no procedure list: a Procedure keeps its description as received (from the pool 20a seeded from AR-35) and an optional RVG code from the group master. Every Booking has a patient with a valid NHI and a payer with an email or postal address (Phase 21), prefilled from the patient or a guardian for a child. Parties (insurers and funders among them, no insurer master) carry their delivery, specialties and funding tags. Contracts are flat, one per priced item, with a pricing kind, price ranges, specialties, at most one tag and the open-price fallbacks. Surgeons sit at one or more rooms, the first the main room (17a). A few stubs sit on Lists for no-NHI intake rows (rooms' PDF rows among them, 34), held outside the Bookings table on their own identifier, never counted as Bookings or in the integrity totals (33a). A few events are billed in the next run (38b). Each Procedure has exactly one Contract chosen by 20's rail: No contract · RVG or a candidate Contract; never a hospital, insurer or procedure default. Its price is typed where set and otherwise follows its Contract (the range in force, the rate or discount as they stand), priced by 24's function; its modifiers are records (age and P1 pre-applied and removable, the odd optional one with its explanation; no ASA pre-fill). Its invoice description comes from 20a's one chain (D37, answered), never a copy made here. Who is billed comes from Phase 21's one function. Insured Bookings carry the approval number; the missing number is the only insurance warning (D28), so billed history raises none. A practice-sized handful (prepaidShare) of Bookings sit on Contracts under Payment type: Prepaid (price ranges above zero, or Prepaid · Fixed price with a typed amount), with a person as payer, the prepayment pair to the AA Holding account and, once AUTHORISED, the transfer to the anaesthetist (Phases 27 and 27a), the prepaid Procedure left off its final invoice with nothing deducted; log the share. Nothing generated carries a removed field (payment category, route, funderOverride, a Contract kind, an estimate or deposit).
- Generated history goes through the same pure builders the billing run and the ledger use (the pricing snapshot written at authorise, invoices priced only from it, one BCTI per receivable invoice, ledger pairs, the holding account's pairs, disbursements on Phase 39a's weekly cycle, Xero mirror with Phase 16's no-personal-information contact labels), never through store actions, and every ledger pair balances. The integrity tests check every Booking has a payer and a valid NHI and exactly one primary Procedure, every Procedure exactly one Contract that fits 20's rule (No contract · RVG or a candidate), its description as received and its pre-applied modifiers, every Contract a pricing kind with at most one tag and one open-price fallback per destination, every AUTHORISED Procedure a snapshot whose price 24's function reproduces (AR-58), every prepaid Booking on a Contract under Payment type: Prepaid with a person as payer, and the AA Holding account in balance with nothing transferred before AUTHORISED and no prepaid Procedure on a final invoice; the AA-FEE invoices are monthly per anaesthetist with the per-BCTI charge from Phase 16's one count function (D1); the audit trail uses only the runtime's own codes (no invented payment or disbursement entries, per US-13.5.2); billed history raises no open warnings; a small deterministic share of authorised Lists carry a problem the Billing monitor recognises. Ids come from the counters, generated invoice numbers use a reserved series, and the next runtime id is unused.
- No Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random() in the data path. performance.now() is for measurement only, lives only in src/store/scaleMode.ts and src/shared/scale/ (everything else, including the leak scan, measures through scaleMode's timed() helper), and never enters AppState.
